# Architecture v2 execution ledger

Branch `arch-v2` in worktree `/Users/arnaud/code/edytor-arch-v2`, based on commit `a7337c5`
(snapshot of `feat/crdt-v14-engine` = d9b7de0 + its uncommitted working tree on 2026-09-26).

Final lanes at C1 (sequential): `check` 0/0 · `lint` clean · `check:worker` OK · `vitest --run` 145 files (3 skipped), 2715 passed, 3 skipped, 11 todo · `test:crdt` 124 files (2 skipped), 2703 passed, 9 skipped (three runs: one lost one row under load, name not captured — the known timing class; two green; seed json restored, no stray `seed-*.doc.json`) · `test:dom` 47 files (1 skipped), 717 passed, 33 skipped · `test:typecheck` 0/0 · `test:dom:typecheck` 0/0 (72/73 errors at R7, fixed in C1) · chromium 523 passed, 1 skipped · firefox 521 passed, 3 skipped · webkit 517 passed, 7 skipped · each desktop engine + the two known reds `composition.spec.ts:563`, `delete-shapes.spec.ts:319` · mobile-chromium 43/43 · mobile-webkit 43/43 · cdp 40/40 · DST 68/68 · `tests/packed-consumer/run.sh` all pass.

**Open after C1.** (1) The exit census is not met on time: the ten `await tick()` sites sit in the input adapters (`events/beforeInputCommands.ts` ×2, `beforeInputDeleteCommands.ts` ×2, `onInput.ts` ×4 with its 20 ms repair wait, `onBeforeInput.ts` ×1) and the observer (×1): V4 left the adapter ones to I1 and I1 did not take them (each is pinned by a mobile or browser spec, so each removal needs its own browser proof). Timers beyond the named rules and the provider/readiness/overlay/heal ones: `onKeyDown`'s native-arrow re-sync (0 ms + rAF + the horizontal fallback), the pointer-drag deferral in `selection.svelte.ts`, the focus-out native-selection clear, the observer's root-heal re-display, `document.ts`'s async-cleanup rethrow. (2) The xloc lands at −33.3 %, above the confident number (18,068): the view rows (`selection`, `events`, root) kept more browser machinery than §7.2 priced, and C1's own step was 21 % short of −350. (3) G-b, G-c and G-d were not taken (D-24). (4) The two known reds stay red on every desktop engine (`composition.spec.ts:563` adopts a jump its harness marks as a gesture — V5 deviation 3; `delete-shapes.spec.ts:319`, a baseline red).

### Known-red chromium specs at baseline (reproduced on rerun)

- `beforeinput-fallback.spec.ts:422` reconciles full text wrapper replacement when input events are missing
- `collaboration-websocket-3client.spec.ts:565` refused schema handshake claims no sync, then recovers
- `composition.spec.ts:565` restores the caret after a delayed browser selection jump following composition
- `delete-shapes.spec.ts:319` deleteSoftLineBackward removes to the soft-line start
- `input.spec.ts:1664` replaces a cross-block browser selection when typing a single character
- `input.spec.ts:1742` replaces a cross-block range that starts in an empty placeholder block
- `input.spec.ts:2479` handles cross-block replacement beforeinput through the model
- `input.spec.ts:3099` pastes multiline text over a live browser selection
- `selection.spec.ts:1362` maps Shift-click range extension across marked text and inline atoms

Gate: a checkpoint may not add a chromium failure outside this list. Fixes to listed specs are recorded.

## Maintainer decisions (§11.2)

The plan's proposed answers are adopted, with these explicit calls:
- Option B (compare-to-truth observer) replaces the render bracket; the vendored engine is an owned fork (plan revision 2).
- D-24: G-a (retire the unexported HTML import plugin) taken; G-e taken; G-d only after a browser check;
  G-b **not taken** (field v13 data cannot be ruled out from the repo); G-c evaluated under the fork.
- D-15 (API retirement) taken as proposed.

## Execution deviations from the plan

- §8 rows are written at the checkpoint that gates them ("Tests first" column), not all at G0.
  G0 writes the infrastructure and the rows gated at G0.

Only the block-selection keys pass it; merges walk the plain order and keep their island rule in `mergeUnnesting` (D3 owns `canMerge`). (2) *Block-selection anchor* (F-P1 needs Shift+Down to shrink): the selected set's first member (insertion order) is the anchor, its last the focus; no new state. **Behavior changes** (all follow from one order): the facade's porous island/void climb stop in `nextInDocOrder` is gone — `mergeForward` on a childless island that is a last child now pulls the following block like its not-last case already did (API-only; unreachable from the caret since island content is not rendered); the view's start-seal in `closestNextBlock` (void/island start with no next sibling → `null`) is gone, so range walks and block-selection moves from such a block continue in document order; ArrowDown from a selected island that is a last child now reaches the block after its parent (was `null`); Shift+Up after select-all shrinks from the end (the focus) instead of no-op; ArrowUp from a selected island-interior block stays inside that island (was: climb to the root; unreachable through handles). **Test-side mirror changed**: `tests/editor-dst/deleteOracle.ts` view-order helpers follow the one order (no island/void climb stop; merges reuse them) — orchestrator: the DST/`delete-shapes` lanes exercise it. **Not in D2**: phantom-returning `firstText/lastText` (L13 lists it; it is a `rendersContent` question — D3/O22); navigation walkers stay on the handle getters (V6, L53); `childrenOf` in placement ops stays (a mid-op snapshot semantic, not an order). |

Red on the reference exactly as predicted (P12 end/start → `paragraph` in the list, middle green; P10 merged-out child → `paragraph`; no `canPlace`/`canMerge`/`defaultChild`/`rendersContent`; no dev report); view F-O6 green on the reference (regression guard). All green after. **Owner**: adopted capability tables on `EdytorDocument` (`roles`, `rendersContent`, `defaultChild`; one two-pass validate-then-apply over all three; `defaultChild(parentType)`, `rendersContent(type)`); facade `canPlace(ids, parent?)`, `canMerge(from, into)`, `defaultChild(parentId)`; view `edytor.defaultChild(parent)`, `Block.movable`, `Block.convertible`, `Block.rendersContent`. **Deleted**: L13 remainder — `canMoveBlockTo`, facade `canAcceptMove`, the extension eligibility predicates (`canConvertBlock` body, `canApplyShortcut` role/`codeLine` checks, `canOpenInBlock`, `canUseHandle`, the handle-host skip), phantom-returning `firstText`/`lastText`, `Block.insideIsland`; the plugin `defaultBlock` hook, `Edytor.getDefaultBlock` and `Edytor.defaultType` (D-13). 358+/286− production lines. **Census**: mechanism counts unchanged (none claimed); type-body 823 → 827 (the two new `DocumentSemanticsConfig` fields and the facade's `defaultChildOf` config doc'd as multi-line types); vendor Δ 0. **Contract readings** (plan silent, recorded): (1) *Where `defaultChild` is declared*: on the parent kind's record (O68, `BlockDefinition.defaultChild`); "merged across extensions" = every extension's declarations are collected before definition precedence applies, so two extensions declaring different default children for one parent type throw `SemanticConflictError` at construction even though definition precedence (D-11, S4) would keep only one record. Both current hook rules were pure functions of the parent type (richText: lists → `list-item`; code: `code` → `codeLine`), so the data-dependent stop condition did not trigger. The code hook's `codeLine → codeLine` answer existed only for the implicit caret-block argument and was dropped. (2) *`canPlace` without a destination* answers "may these blocks move at all" (the drag affordance); *convert eligibility* (convert, markdown shortcut, slash menu) = `canPlace([id])` ∧ no role of its own — the `codeLine` exclusions were already implied by the island seal. The toolbar's `!state.isVoid && !state.isIsland` reads the selection projection, not a re-derived rule, and stays (V1). (3) *`canMoveBlocks` request shape*: readonly, the position, the target being live and not one of the moved blocks stay view-side request validation; everything structural is `canPlace`. (4) *`firstText`/`lastText`* = the block's own content's first/last text; a kind declaring `rendersContent: false` has none (not its children's — descending into a container's last item regressed `del.range.nested-tail`, whose range-end canonicalization walks to the previous block's own last text); only a block with no content slot (the root) descends. Block-selection state keeps a leaf void's slot as its model endpoint through one explicit `edgeText` helper in `selection.svelte.ts` until V2 stores block sets by id. (5) *Dev check* = `Block.svelte` records the snippet key under which `content()` rendered (via `Content`'s `onrender`) and compares it with the declaration in an `$effect` after the render; reported once per kind per editor with `console.warn('[edytor] …')`; both directions are reported. **Tests changed under D-13**: `dom/beforeinput` "routes code-line insertLineBreak…", `dom/plugins` "routes shift+enter in code lines…", `model/hotkeys` "routes shift+enter in code lines…" (the split tail is `codeLine`: before, richText's catch-all `defaultBlock` returned `paragraph` first in plugin order); `model/pipeline` D16 fixtures call `defaultChild(parent)` instead of `getDefaultBlock` (same answers; the implicit caret-block argument is gone by P12). **Test-side mirror changed**: the DST delete oracle resets island merge-out children to the captured `defaultChild` of the new parent (`browserState` captures `document.semantics.defaultChild` and `edytor.defaultChild(root)` instead of the removed `defaultType`/`getDefaultBlock`) — orchestrator: DST lanes exercise it. **Behavior notes**: Enter at the start/end of a list item and `addChildBlock()`/selected-block replacement without a type now use the actual parent's default child; an island merged out into a list yields list items; a markdown `---` conversion writes no caret into the divider; a second view whose kinds declare a different `rendersContent` or `defaultChild` for an adopted type is refused. |

`structs.ts` `clockOf`/`deletedLen`); `OpResult {status, ids, reason?}` exported; `setIfChanged` (placement) is the one same-value guard; attribution and lineage are written by ONE pass when the outermost op closes, over the structs it wrote (registry insert → `createdBy`; content/claims/type/data → contributor + `l`; placement and delete marks → nothing; split inheritance and merge unions are the two recorded intents); ingress: every facade op normalizes its inputs once (`sanitizeSpec` moved to `utils/json.ts`, `ref` for ids; reads normalize through `byRef`; `block(id)` normalizes), the placement and text layers trust their inputs; `insertBlocks` joins the facade; `moveBlock`/`nestBlock`/`unNestBlock` are `moveBlocks`; every `DocBlock` mutator returns the facade's `OpResult`. **Deleted**: L11 — `marksAlready`, the `deleteText` clamp and `insertText('')` re-derivations, same-value type/data/inline-data pre-checks, the `setBlock` `wrote` accumulation, per-op `lineagePending`/`commitLineage`/`stampTouched`/`stampCreatedSpec`/`stampMerged` threading; L15 (facade half) — facade policy wrappers repeating model preludes, per-kid moves in `mergeUnnesting`/`deleteBlock(keepChildren)` (one `moveBlocks`, identical rank stream), the `nodes.ts` boolean→handle conversion layer, `subtreeJSON` (second subtree serializer; `blockJSON` is the one and `toJSON` uses it); F20 ingress sites (`buildInline`, `materializeSpec` clones, split newId/tail, `setInlineData`, `insertIntoText`, `formatRangeIn`, `stampSpecTree`, `duplicateBlock`). 554+/621− code lines (720+/903− with comments). **Census**: mechanism counts unchanged (none claimed); type-body 827 → 823; vendor Δ 0. **Tests changed** (result shape, R6/§6 D20 and the D4 row's "one result shape for every op"): boolean verdicts rewritten to statuses with the same verdict (`true` → `applied`, `false`/`null` → `refused`, returned ids → `.ids`) in 12 files; the harness `doc-ops` adapter maps not-refused to `true` (its cross-backend contract stays boolean); three pins of the C8 inconsistency now read `noop` per F-D5 (empty-range format was `false`; same-value `setInlineData` and mark-free `clearMarks` were `true`). No other expectation changed. **Behavior notes**: a split at a block's end no longer stamps or captures lineage for the source (its content did not change — R6's effect set decides); stamping is per outermost op, not per `facade.transact` (raw model writes inside a `transact` stay unattributed, as `block-lineage` pins; once-per-outer-transaction is O19's D10 gate); a same-value island reset in `mergeUnnesting` writes nothing; an op refused for an absent target (dead block, unknown inline id) is `refused`, an empty or clamped-away range is `noop`. |

(2) *Outside the range survives*: the tail's children and the later siblings inside every container the range dies through take the topmost such container's slot, whether or not the head survives; a merging tail's children take its vacated slot (`mergeBackward`'s placement; an island tail's children take the slot's default child). (5) *Positions* name shown text: a block that is not live, has no content node or declares `rendersContent: false` is refused. Revised rule: the op never canonicalizes; a range ending at the tail's start covers the seam, so the tail's whole text is its suffix and joins a surviving head (`canMerge` permitting); with an empty head prefix the head dies and the tail survives whole with its id (the pinned nested `alpha@0 → beta@0`); the tail dies iff its suffix is empty. The contract's `[a@2→c@0] → "aa","cc"` / `[a@0→b@0] → "","bb","cc"` are the selection's canonical form of those ranges (`[a@2→b@2]`, `[a@0→a@2]`) — contract row rewritten; the DST oracle follows (1,290 random cases agree with the op in a temporary differential). (b) `hotkeys.spec.ts:1296` — undo after typing over a cross-block range restored a collapsed caret: the undo snapshot was taken at `stack-item-added` (after the write), and its anchors bound the atoms the claim merge moved into the head. F-M4's command half (`dispatcher.last.status === 'failed'`) replaces the D5 `todo`. **Owner**: `Dispatcher` (`src/lib/session/commands.ts`), `edytor.dispatcher` (the name `commands` is taken by the plugin command map). `dispatch(operation, payload, context, body, prepare?)`: permission (`readonly`, `document.writable`) → `prepare` (operations that are one document plan: `splitBlock`, `setBlock`, `moveBlock`, `moveBlocks`, `deleteContentWithinSelection`, `insertFlow`, and the new `deleteBlocks`) → `onBeforeOperation` on the command, then on each planned step mapped to its documented name (`deleteBlock`→`removeBlock`, `mergeBlocks`→`mergeBlockBackward`, `deleteText`→`deleteContentAtRange`, `insertBlocks`→`addChildBlocks`, `moveBlocks`→`moveBlock`/`moveBlocks`, `splitBlock`, `setBlockType`/`setBlockData`→`setBlock`, `insertText`, `insertInline`→`addInlineBlock`) → one transaction (operations issued inside it are its steps: no hooks) → normalization (`normalizeContent`/`normalizeChildren` requested inside a command are queued, deduplicated and drained once at the end of its transaction) → `last` (`refused | noop | applied | failed`) → `onAfterOperation` once, original payload. `run(kind, body)` = a user command: permission, the undo policy table (`CUT`: deletions, paste, drop, structural and format commands cut before; `insertParagraph` also after; a command inside a command is its step), one prevention scope. `scope`/`intercept` = prevention scopes for event-level hooks (`onBeforeInput`, `onCopy`/`onCut`/`onPaste`, hotkeys, keydown); a veto of an operation inside a scope aborts the scope, its replacement runs after. `PreventionError.by` names the preventing extension. **Deleted**: L37 — the 7 `instanceof PreventionError` catch sites (`onBeforeInput`, `onKeyDown`, `onCut`, `onCopy`, `onPaste`, `hotkeys.isHotkey`, `BlockHandleController.moveAndSelect`) and the hotkey catch-all that reported every error as handled; `runOperation` (block.utils) and both interception loops; L38 — the 19 `stopCapturing()` decisions (hotkeys ×9, richTextOperations ×3, onKeyDown, onCut, onPaste ×2, onInput, `beforeInputCommands`' after-Enter `finally`, `stopHistoryCaptureIfNeeded`) → the table; `removeSelectedBlocksForReplacement`'s bare `transact(facade.deleteBlocks)` → the `deleteBlocks` operation. 346+/390− lines in touched production files + 359 in the new module. **Census**: `instanceof PreventionError` 7 → **1**, `stopCapturing()` 19 → **1** (both in the dispatcher); others unchanged (setTimeout 41, rAF 8, `await tick()` 29, `flushMirror(` 20, `ignoreNextSelectionChange` 21, MutationObserver 4, `flushSync(` 0); type-body 881 → 880; vendor Δ +27 (unchanged). **Where the step went**: the plan's +75 books the dispatcher against deletions it did not find at that size — the catch sites and `stopCapturing` sites were one to eight lines each (−~90 across event files), `runOperation` −45; the module is 269 (hooks/permission/transaction/normalization queue ≈150, the step adapter ≈70 = the plan's "adapters for the documented operation names", `specJSON`/`partAt` ≈25). Reported, not redesigned (no special case beyond the contract). **Contract readings** (recorded): (1) *After-hooks* — F-O3's "every hook call before the transaction" is read for `onBeforeOperation`; `onAfterOperation` runs once per command after its transaction (never inside). (2) *Dedup* — a planned step that is the command itself (same documented name, same block) is not shown twice (the D5 pipeline fixtures pin one `moveBlocks` before-call). (3) *Created blocks* — a write into a block the plan creates is part of that creation (as D7's `effectOf`), so a paste's tail text is not shown; the tail's split is. (4) *Step coordinates* — steps are shown on the block they name at prepare time: D6 plans delete a merged tail's prefix in the head after the merge, so F-O3's range row expects two `deleteContentAtRange` on the head (the S1 test first expected one on the tail; corrected in the implementation commit). (5) *Scope abort* — a veto of an operation issued by a user command aborts the rest of that command synchronously (today's semantics); writes already made by earlier operations of a multi-operation ladder stay until S2 turns the ladders into single plans. (6) *Normalization once per touched parent* = each (block, normalizer) request inside a command runs once at its end; the requests stay where today's operations make them (plan effects do not add new ones). (7) *Errors surface*: `withUserInput` swallowed async handler rejections (`Promise.resolve(p).catch(() => {})` handled the original promise); it now reports them (`reportError`, else `console.error`) — F-M1's "the error surfaces". (8) *Composition capture group* is I3's; the table leaves `deleteCompositionText` in its group. (9) *Snapshot queueing* (L38's second half) stays: the undo selection snapshots are selection values (V2/S7); the block-selection one moved into the `deleteBlocks` body so a refused delete queues none. **Document change**: `insertBlocks`/`moveBlocks` plan steps carry `index` (the destination slot at prepare time, as hooks see it). **Plugin API changes** (K5, README "Prevention in Plugin Operations"): hooks see planned steps; a nested veto refuses the whole command; nested payload rewrites are ignored (dev warning); a replacement is re-prepared and shown to every hook, once per extension; `normalizeContent`/`normalizeChildren` inside a command are no longer shown as operations; nested operations inside an operation are not shown; `onAfterOperation` fires once per command; top-level operations no longer throw `PreventionError` to the caller (they report `refused`); text operations refuse in readonly; a read-only document refuses instead of throwing; new operation name `deleteBlocks` (`{blocks, snapshot?}`). **Tests changed**: `plugin-branches` code-line merge vetoes expected a thrown `PreventionError` → `dispatcher.last.status === 'refused'` (mechanism pin, D-10); `hotkeys.test.ts` gives its bare editor a `Dispatcher`. **Chromium note for the orchestrator**: async handler errors are now reported (`pageerror`/console) instead of swallowed — a spec whose `trackPageIssues` newly fails is a previously hidden failure; pasting or dropping text that a plugin intercepts by `insertText` (e.g. a lone `@` with the mention plugin) is intercepted again, as before D7; F-M5 (b) (`input.spec:1023`) and `history-coalesced-range.spec` exercise the policy table. |

D-16 — the `selection` mirror, `startTextId`/`endTextId`/`yStart`/`yEnd`, the renderer's id/offset fallback. **Census**: mechanisms unchanged (V2 claims none: setTimeout 41, rAF 8, `await tick()` 29, `flushMirror(` 20, `stopCapturing()` 1, `instanceof PreventionError` 1, `ignoreNextSelectionChange` 21, MutationObserver 4, `flushSync(` 0); type-body 922 → 916; vendor Δ +27 (unchanged). V1 shadow classes at V2: 1 `dom-string-content` (F-S6 content half) — gone, `state.content` is the model slice now; 2 `stale-derived-field` (F-S8) — gone, every field is a projection of (value, version); 3 `blocks-deleted-under-block-selection` — remote: repaired (F-S7), local: repaired to the seam, then the command's own caret (F-S13 stays V3); 4 `block-set-changed-without-state` — gone (F-S3); 5 `local-command-caret-pending` — unchanged (V3, orchestrator answer 2). **Deviations**: (1) `select()` lives on the view (`selection.svelte.ts`): the reactive holder needs runes; `session/selection.ts` keeps the pure parts (`project`, `sameValue`, `serialize`, `SelectCause`). (2) New counter `Edytor.mirrorRevision` (bumped by `flushMirror` after the mirror reconciles, before the repair): the compat getter maps to wrappers that lag the index inside a flush; keying only on the index version cached an empty state for F-S8. (3) The deferred-write staleness checks (`setAtTextOffset`/`setAtRange`/`setAtBlockRange`/history restore/verification), the undo snapshot and the Android snap-back read `#written` — the state as the last `select()` wrote it — not the projection: they mean "did another writer run", and undo snapshots taken at `stack-item-added` must stay the pre-edit selection (a projection already followed the edit: P1-3 failed on it). Both go with V4/S7. (4) Emission is by value: a remote edit that moves the projection no longer emits or republishes (peers resolve the anchors); focus/blur hooks no longer churn on every derive. (5) `selection.state` has no setter: 4 test files that assigned it (and 2 that `Object.assign`ed it) use `setRangeStateAtTextOffsets` / `select(noSelection)`; the headless harness `createTestEdytor` now writes a real (non-forced-collapsed) value. (6) V2a is 7 % over the ≈1,500-line guide. **Contract readings** (recorded): (a) an equal `select()` re-applies the effects and emits nothing; (b) "the block the caret left" = a block holding the previous selection's start or end and neither of the new ones; (c) block-set and atom presence entries are published but not rendered as remote carets (L10 names anchors only); (d) `createAwarenessSelection` keeps its name and returns the new payload. **Public API**: `selection.state` read-only (same fields; `yTextContent` gone; block set `blocks` = selected ids); new `selection.value`, `select`, `projection`, `epoch`, `cause`, `suggestions`, `textValue`; removed `hasSelectedAll`, `focusBlocks`; presence wire per D-16 (`EdytorAwarenessSelection` = `{start, end, collapsed, reversed}`, `EdytorAwarenessViewSelection` = `PresenceSelection & {t}`). **Gate follow-up (9ebc58e)**: (i) `tests/editor-dom/collaboration.spec.ts:5` read the removed `selection` mirror — re-pinned to D-16 (the fake peer copies the local `selections` entry); the renderer draws `[data-edytor-remote-cursor][data-client-id="9001"]` labelled Ada from `selections` on chromium, firefox and webkit (12/12). (ii) DST solo seed 7 (multiline-rich-blocks) step 3, `cross-browser-selection-divergence [firefox/chromium]`: Firefox commits `keyboard.insertText` through a composition; once the replacement deleted the REVERSED range it projected collapsed, `isReversed` fell to false and the compat `relativePosition` became the value's `anchor` — the END, bound left to a deleted atom — instead of the start; the composition pin re-reads `relativePosition` after every write, so the region began after the inserted `é` and the caret landed at 2 (chromium, no composition: 1). Owner fix: `anchorsInOrder(value, projection)` in `session/selection.ts` — anchors in (start, end) order, keeping their minted roles (start binds right, end binds left) when an edit left no order; used by the compat getter and `serialize`. Rows: `arch-v2-v2-select.test.tsx` › typing over a reversed range of marked runs via composition (red before the fix: caret 2) and via `beforeinput` (pin) → `é`·`ords`, caret 1. Pins: a vetoed block-set delete keeps the block set; cut through the harness; a collapsed toggle after / before the caret block; the undeclared phantom `panel` (D3 kinds) before / after; `sel.seam.*` through the view (nested subtree, adjacent dead siblings, last block); a local op that declares no result still gets the seam; a range write whose block dies while it waits → seam; V1 shadow class 5 (a collapsed Backspace's value is its caret in its own turn — green since V2's projection: the class closed at V2, pinned here). **Owner**: `seam(doc, dead, displayable)` in `src/lib/crdt/anchors.ts` — climb while the dead block's display parent is dead (`facade.slotOf`: the replicated `{p, r}` of any registered block, display parent or the raw parent when that one is dead); among the live parent's visible children (`facade.childSlots`, `(rank, id)` order) the first displayable stop at/after the slot (start), else the last before it (end); with neither, one level up (the parent's own content is then a stop before the slot); `null` when nothing displays. `EdytorSelection.#displayable(id)` = the block's own content has a mounted text not inside `[hidden], details:not([open]) > :not(summary)` (the Surface fact, §2.4); `#seamValue` mints the caret through `anchorAt`. `restoreDeadSelectionEndpoints` repairs only endpoints this view did not author: it returns while `dispatcher.authoring` is set; the seam origin is the block id the value last resolved in (`#lastBlock`, now an id), a dead block set's first id, or a vanished atom's block. `Dispatcher.caret(text, offset, ops?)` is the one result-selection writer: with `ops` the caret value is minted before the operations (so it survives them) and declared as `authoring`; it is selected once when they applied, recorded as `CommandResult.selection`, then displayed through `setAtTextOffset` (V4); if the declared caret did not survive, the seam repair runs. Block-set delete and cut share `deleteSelectedBlocks` (`replaceSelection.ts`): FP-7's previous-then-next unselected block, its first editable text's end; with neither, no result is declared and the seam applies. Deferred writes (`setAtTextOffset`, `setAtRange`) mint their value at call time and fall back through `#admit(intended, origin)` (the value when it projects, else the seam of the origin's block), still behind their gesture-serial `stale()` gates. **Deleted**: L25 — `Block._dropNext/_dropPrev` and their capture in `_drop` (−10), the wrapper seam walk `#seamOf` (moved to `doc/anchors`, 70 → 47); L21 (V3 part) — the dead-endpoint application's seam/land/pending branches (rewritten, ~−25 net), `writeCollapsedCaretState`, `writeRangeCaretState`, `resolveDeadCaretTarget` (−45 → `#admit` + call-time minting ≈20); `focusDeletedBlockFallback` (onCut), `RemovedSelectedBlocks.blockToFocus` and its `parent.children[index] ?? … ?? root.children[0]` fallback (the command declares its result or the seam applies); the intermediate `selectBlocks()` of the block-set delete (it stays on the replacement path). **Census**: mechanisms unchanged (setTimeout 38, rAF 8, `await tick()` 28, `flushMirror(` 15, `stopCapturing()` 1, `instanceof PreventionError` 1, `ignoreNextSelectionChange` 19, MutationObserver 4, `flushSync(` 0); type-body 927 → 932 (`SeamDoc`, `CommandResult.selection`); vendor Δ +27 (unchanged). **Where the step went** (rule 7): the plan's −220 books L21's "dead-endpoint application" at 118 and `writeCollapsed…` at 60 from the reader's pre-V2 measure. V2 already rewrote the application through `select()` (its −503 includes the removed focus branching and `applyState`-without-emit; ≈110 lines of the seam walk were consolidated into `#seamOf` there), so V3 found ≈150 xloc left in its rows, of which the seam (47, moved, as planned: L25 ~81 → 45) and the repair's surviving REQUIRED half (block set / atom / text branches, the pending flag and the focused re-display — both V4's, AGENTS contracts) stay; the new owner costs +19 in the dispatcher and +8 facade reads. The remaining L21 (`restoreRelativePosition`, capture/reconcile, 204) and L22 are V4's; L25's two history redo fallbacks (`restoreDeletedSelectionFallback` in the pop handler, `undoRestore.ts` redo fallback) are left to S7, which rewrites `history/*` in parallel (L24). Reported, not redesigned: the rows are the plan's and green. **Deviations**: (1) the seam is applied by the repair's `select('repair')`, not inside `project()`: the displayable filter is a Surface fact and F-S2 needs one emission (a new value), so the projection stays DOM-free and the value is replaced once. (2) Past the sibling level the rule continues outward in document order (next stop after the live parent, else the parent's own content) instead of E3's "first editable text of the document" — the latter would jump over the nearest visible stop (F-S14 "next displayable stop"); identical to E3 whenever a displayable sibling exists; recorded as `sel.seam.slot` in `docs/editor-delete-contract.md`. (3) ~~The backward stop is the last displayable stop in document order~~ — reverted by the gate fix: the backward stop is the previous sibling's own content end, then its children last first (`sel.seam.next-sibling`'s "previous sibling's end", as `lastEditableText` and the collab DST oracle read it). (4) `doc/anchors` lives at `src/lib/crdt/anchors.ts` (the D-checkpoints' convention, e.g. `crdt/rangeDelete.ts`). **Contract readings** (recorded): (a) "authored" = declared before the operations (`Dispatcher.caret(…, ops)`); a command that writes its caret after its ops (the S2 `caret(text, offset)` callers, the `await setAtTextOffset` ladders) did not author the endpoints its ops killed, so the seam repairs them first and the command's caret follows (today's order, one extra emission only when the command killed its own endpoint); converting those callers is per-command work for I1/V4. (b) Displayable = mounted own content ∧ not inside a closed `<details>` body or a `[hidden]` subtree; an extension that hides content some other way declares `hidden` (no new extension API). **AGENTS.md selection contracts**: both stay in force and are unchanged — `deadEndpointRecoveryPending` is still armed when the seam finds no displayable stop and completed by `Text.attach` (`notifyTextMounted`); every deferred write's fallback (`#admit`) still runs only after its gesture-serial `stale()` check. The seam's displayable filter is strictly stronger than `firstEditableText`'s mounted-node requirement (phantom slots stay skipped). "Never let a caret migrate into a surviving neighbour" is untouched (anchor resolution). **Gate follow-up (cd8cc9d)**: two collaboration regressions found by the orchestrator's full chromium and DST runs, both "seam before atom-following" / "seam order" defects of V3, fixed in their owners. (i) chromium `collaboration-websocket.spec.ts:671` (a remote merge of the caret block; a caret write then aimed at the captured, now dead text) landed at the seam's next sibling `collab-b3` instead of the merged-into `collab-b1`: V3's call-time minting used `textValue`, whose `createTextAnchor` refuses a dead wrapper, so the write fell straight to the seam. Fix: deferred writes mint their intent through `#intent` = `textValue` with a minter that reads the text's block record live or dead (`anchorAt(blockId, segStart + offset)`, what `resolveDeadCaretTarget` did), so anchor resolution follows the claimed atoms first and the seam applies only when nothing resolves (`#admit`). (ii) collab DST seed 3 (`inline-atom-boundaries`, chromium, 3 peers, `remote-selection-misplaced`): the caret's block died last in its parent; the previous sibling had its own text (`·!.·é`, 5) and a nested child (`c8·é`, 4); V3's reverse-pre-order backward stop took the child's end (`@4`), the contract/oracle ("the previous sibling's end" = its own content's end) expects `@5`. Fix: `seam`'s backward stop = own content end if displayable, else children last first. Rows (red on baf3561, green after): doc `v3-seam.test.ts` › the previous sibling end is its own content end, before its children; dom `arch-v2-v3-seam.test.tsx` › a caret write aimed at a text that already died to a merge follows its atoms into the merged-into block (live text), not the seam. (2) *`after`* (coordinator answer b): the selection the step's last transaction left, followed through the same gesture's `select()`s; a newer gesture closes it. Without a gesture in between (headless code), a later programmatic `select()` still counts as the step's result. (3) *Remote-apply re-assert*: `captureSelectionForRemoteApply` returns nothing for the replay's own commit, and `reconcileSelectionAfterRemoteApply` stands down when a non-DOM `select()` (model, repair, history) ran after its capture (new `#modelEpoch`) — the replaced latch had covered this. (4) *Browser-owned input*: the reconcile first `select()`s the value the input was admitted with (recorded at `beforeinput` on `browserOwnedInputTarget.selection`), so a native selection jump before `input` does not become the step's `before` (`input.spec:1023`); this replaces the queued `historyOffset` override and belongs to I1's attempt record when it lands. (5) The api channel (`edytor.historyUndo()`) now also records the redo side (it previously did not queue the restore snapshot: F-U9 api red). **Contract answers** (coordinator): (a) keep an entry on every tracked stack item (deviation 1 stands); (b) `after` = the selection when the step's last transaction ended (the fix). |

Browser `tests/editor-dom/arch-v2-v4-projector.spec.ts` (7 × 3 engines): F-S6; F-S9 (a) remount keeps the caret and focus (red on all three), (b), (c); F-S11 (a) ArrowRight with a peer write in the keydown capture microtask (red on firefox), (b) drag under 60 Hz peer writes (pin), (c) `setBaseAndExtent` racing a peer write (red on all three). cdp (`ime-baseline.cdp.spec.ts`): five new green rows "no DOM-selection write while the session lives (F-S12, BI-2)" — mid-word, empty block, bold, peer same block, peer other block (the reference called `Selection` mutators several times per session, `removeAllRanges`+`addRange` pairs). **Owner**: `surface/projector.svelte.ts` — `pre()` from the root `$effect.pre` (notes the focused element), `post()` from the root `$effect` (runs after every DOM write of the flush; deps: `selection.request`, `render`, `editorDomRevision`, `valueRevision`, `isComposing`), a pass keyed on (request, render epoch, remount): skipped while composing (BI-2; the session end re-runs it), for a pass nobody requested while a drag or a user-input frame is live; a value that no longer projects runs the seam repair and waits; the live DOM selection is compared with the value in model coordinates (`getTextOfNode` + `getYIndex`, direction included) and only a difference is written, after the focus verdict (focus inside, or orphaned by our render — the noted element detached or the selection collapsed onto an ancestor of the root; never a foreign focus or a selection outside the editor; with `body` focused only a requested display writes, so a background pass never takes focus), focusing the editing host first (never a link inside the text); `setBaseAndExtent` for backward ranges; scroll only for a request made in a user-input frame (`scrollOnDisplay`); block sets and atoms clear an in-editor DOM selection; the shown nodes and mark-edge side are recorded as the value's DOM-derived fields (`selection.observed`). Render epoch: commits this view did not issue, a text (re)mount holding the selection or while a display waits, the observer's records signal while a display waits. BI-3: `beforeTransaction` of a transaction whose origin is neither this view's nor its undo manager, after a settled render (`facade.version` = the last rendered) and with no display pending: the live DOM selection is minted (`selection.mint`, anchors only) when it projects elsewhere than the value; `afterTransaction` admits it through `select(…, 'dom')`, outside the transaction (LH2-5). `select()`: `cause` model/history → `display()` (request, `requestSerial`, scroll frame); `repair` → a background pass (render epoch); `dom` → none. `setAtTextOffset`/`setAtRange`/`setAtBlockRange`/`setAtTextsRange` select their value in their turn (anchors minted from the block record, else the seam — V3's `#admit`). **Minimal classification kept in V4 to retire the echo setters** (the rest is V5's): an echo is the last display unchanged (points, same selection epoch; after a clear for a block set or an atom, anything until the next gesture — Chromium parks a caret at the document start after `removeAllRanges` in a focused host); a `selectionchange` is not intent while a requested display has not landed and no intent gesture came since the request, or while the value is displayed, no intent gesture came since, and the observer holds unreconciled records (foreign damage — its repair displays again); `restoreDriftedEchoCaret` additionally needs a flush since the last observation (F-S4). `Edytor.intentSerial` = the gesture serial without `input` events (an `input` records what the browser did, not where the user wants the selection). **Deleted** — L22: `setAtTextOffset`/`setAtRange`/`setAtBlockRange` bodies (staleness closures, 10-attempt mount loops, lookup-failure fallbacks), `setAtTextsRange`'s DOM write, `scheduleCaretWriteVerification` + `SELECTION_WRITE_VERIFY_DELAY_MS` + `caretWriteEpoch`, `pendingBlockRangeRequest`, `stateMatchesSelectionTarget`, `setAtNodeOffset`, `findTextNode`, `foreignFocusOwnsSelection`, `restoreBackwardDomRange`, the fabricated-snapshot derive after each write, `deadEndpointRecoveryPending` + `notifyTextMounted`, `#land`'s display, `#written` (the Android arming evidence is now `#caret`, three fields), `domSelectionIsCollapsedAt`; L21 (rest): `restoreRelativePosition` (+ `Text._setItems` call, blurred-selection repair with its `tick` + 0/50 ms re-asserts), `captureSelectionForRemoteApply`/`reconcileSelectionAfterRemoteApply` (+ the `onChange` capture and `tick` reconcile), `#modelEpoch`, `scheduleReassert`, `isEditorFocused`; L26 core: `Edytor.getTextNode` (mount polling; the test harness has `textNodeOf`), the structural-hotkey caret and block-selection restores (tick + 30 + 500 ms each), the block-set delete focus re-assertion (tick + 30 ms), the onInput 30 ms re-assert, `Dispatcher.caret`'s trailing write, `history`'s explicit `display()`; L52: the toolbar's numeric snapshot and its model+DOM re-assertion (it holds `selection.value`), the slash menu's numeric range (trigger and query end are `TextAnchor`s resolved per read); the `ignoreNextSelectionChange` setters of display echoes (structural caret, mod+a, triple-click write, atom selection in navigation, onInput ×2) and the redundant `setCollapsedStateAtTextOffset` before writes. Also fixed: `isBackward` compares boundary points (an ancestor focus `(textElement, childCount)` is after its anchor — F-S6 on chromium once the projector displays the derived value), `getYIndex` skips the outside-element boundary rule for a point inside the text element (F-S5). **Census** (ref → V4a → V4b): `setTimeout(` 37 → 35 → 29 · rAF 8 → 8 → 8 · `await tick()` 20 → 14 → 14 · `flushMirror(` 15 · `stopCapturing()` 1 · `instanceof PreventionError` 1 · `ignoreNextSelectionChange` writes 15 → 13 → 7 (left: pointer-originated ignores — chrome pointerdown, triple click, atom pointerdown ×2 — and the resets: V5) · MutationObserver 4 · `flushSync(` 0 · type-body 932 → 932 · vendor 7,152 (Δ +27, unchanged). **AGENTS.md selection contracts retired, with the invariant that replaces each**: (1) "a live destination with no mounted node arms `deadEndpointRecoveryPending` and is completed by `Text.attach`" → *the projector's pass after every flush retries what did not land: a value that does not project runs the seam repair (the root `$effect` of the flush that mounts the cell sees it), a display whose destination is not mounted waits, and a text mount or the observer's records signal re-runs the pass while one waits; the seam's displayable filter still skips phantom slots.* (2) "every deferred selection write checks the gesture serial before writing — all argument forms and all exits" → *there is no deferred write: every writer selects its value in its own turn and the projector always writes the CURRENT value after the flush, so an older request can never overwrite a newer one; a native move either was derived (then it is the value) or is minted before any foreign transaction (BI-3); while a requested display has not landed, a `selectionchange` with no intent gesture since the request is not adopted.* The anchor contract (`{b, a, o?}`, never migrating into a neighbour) is unchanged. **Deviations**: (1) *V4a over the guide*: the deferred writers and the remote-apply repairers are one mechanism (the repairers call the writers). A V4a that kept `restoreRelativePosition`/reconcile beside the projector was built and measured: `command-schedules` seeds 7, 42 and 55 lost in-process determinism (trace mismatch) — no green hybrid exists, so V4a carries both (1,164 of its 1,810 lines are deletions in one file); V4b is the separable rest. (2) *F-S12 re-attributed*: the display half is green (zero `Selection` mutator calls while a session lives, all five scenarios); the restart half stays `test.fail`, relabelled "F-S12 restart half (I3 pin: the render rewrites the IME node)": with no selection write left, Chromium still restarts the composition because the host text node is rewritten under the IME after the first update — mid-word the pinned render writes the preview at the host's end (`noteｋ` over the IME's `noｋte`), at the end it rewrites identical text, and either write resets the caret (§1.1 text-writer fact); a MutationObserver shows the second `characterData` write, then `compositionstart` again. The ledger's "Infra: cdp lane" diagnosis (selection writes end the composition) was one of two causes. (3) *Classification in V4*: the echo/awaited rules and the drift rule's flush condition above are the minimum that retires the echo setters without regressions (`inline-atomic.spec` atom arrows, `beforeinput-fallback.spec:724`, `mobile-beforeinput.spec:759`); V5 owns the named rules and the rest of the classifier. (4) *Focus verdict*: `lastUserGestureOutsideEditor` is not read; the gesture landing acts through "a background pass never writes with `body` focused" and "never over a selection outside the editor" — a requested display with `body` focused writes (the demo's block-menu "Add block below" after a click outside: `demo-route.spec:276`). A repair is a background pass (it never takes focus; F-S2). (5) *Dedupe key*: (request counter, render epoch, remount revision) instead of (selection epoch, render epoch): DOM-derived selects advance the epoch without asking for a display. (6) Records signal (O55) wired minimally: the observer's flush ends with `projector.recordsChanged` (a render bump only while a display waits), and its model-owned-input heal asks for a display. (7) Tests adjusted: `elegance-selection` D7 (the deferred-write staleness pins become "a user move after a request in the same turn wins", model and DOM; the lookup-failure fallbacks become "the model write lands in the request's turn"), `arch-v2-s7-history` F-U7 focuses V1 before placing its caret (jsdom does not move focus with a selection; V2's caret is now displayed), `selection-write-dedupe` drops the retired helper, `createTestEdytor` stops patching `setAtNodeOffset`. (8) Left to I1 (conflict avoidance): the `await tick()` before caret writes in `events/*` (they now wait for nothing) and `onBeforeInput`'s `tick().then(setAtTextOffset)`; left to V5: `stabilizeCompositionSelection` (IME post-commit jump), the Android snap-back; pointer code stays in `selection.svelte.ts` (`surface/pointer.ts` not split out: no owner change). **Contract questions**: (a) confirm F-S12's restart half moves to I3 (the IME-node pin must stop rendering the preview into the IME's node — Svelte's writer rewrites it even when identical). (b) AGENTS.md "Selection contracts that must not regress" still states the two retired bullets and `docs/editor-delete-contract.md` "Selection ownership and lifecycle" still describes deferred writes: rewrite them with the invariants above (not done here). (c) `intentSerial` (gestures but `input`) is a V4 stand-in for "an occurrence that says where the user wants the selection" — I1's attempts may own it. (d) The echo-after-clear rule (a display that cleared the DOM selection for a block set or an atom ignores what the engine parks there until the next gesture) — keep as a V5 named rule? |

Full integration and DST: the orchestrator runs them | **Rows** (`src/tests/fixtures/dom/arch-v2-t6-presence.test.tsx`, dom lane, 6 tests). F-T10: two mounted views of one document each publish a caret, and view 1's `selection.destroy` is stubbed out (the teardown path that skips it). Unmounting view 1 removes its entry at once with exactly one `setLocalState`, and the sibling entry keeps its `t`. On the reference the entry stayed (`['view-1','view-2']`, C14). R1: a sibling's publish never removes another view's entry, even one whose view is flagged `destroyed`. On the reference the sweep removed it. Both were expected-fail in c843d8d and each failed for the predicted reason. Pins, green before and after: a live `refreshEditorDom` remount keeps the entry with no broadcast; a destroyed view never publishes; one wire shape per kind (`{start,end,collapsed,reversed,t}`, `{blocks,t}`) with no `selection` field; a remote client with two valid entries and one junk entry with a high `t` renders exactly one caret. **Owner**: the view. `Edytor.presenceKey = mintPresenceKey()`. `select()` calls `publishPresence(awareness, key, serialize(value, projection))` when the value changed and the view is alive. `Edytor.destroy()` calls `publishPresence(awareness, key, null)` right after setting `destroyed`, so it does not depend on `selection.destroy()` running. `selection.destroy()` no longer touches presence. `publishPresence` is the one write: it copies other keys untouched and dedupes only when the entry minus `t` deep-equals the previous entry (an identical republish keeps its `t`, G23). It drops the field when no entry is left and keeps `actor`/`user`. **Anchor resolution in one place**: `resolvePeerSelection(edytor, state)` in `awarenessSelection.ts` takes the freshest valid text entry per client, applies the wire-shape guard (`isTextAnchor`, moved in from `remoteSelection.ts`) and resolves both anchors, or returns `null` so nothing is painted. `remoteSelection.ts` keeps only geometry. **Deleted (L59, the rest)**: the `viewPresenceIds` WeakMap and counter, the `presenceOwners` registry with `ownersOf`, `readSelections`, `sweepDestroyedViews`, `writePresenceFields`, `createAwarenessSelection`, `clearAwarenessSelection`, `publishedEntryEquals` with the final `jsonValuesEqual` guard, `publishAwarenessSelection`, the renderer's `getSelection`/`resolveRelativePosition` copies, and the `RemoteSelections` wrapper closure. Production diff: 6 files, collaboration 513 → 412 (awareness 181 → 131, remote 213 → 166, overlay 83 → 80, index 13 → 12), `edytor.svelte.ts` +3, `selection.svelte.ts` −1. **What V2 already took of L59**: the legacy `textId/yStart` fields, the `selection` mirror, the numeric and mirror fallbacks, and publishing `serialize(value)` (collaboration −108), plus the selection-side `emittedStatesMatch` dedupe, which is booked under L20. **Shortfall**: (1) `findDomPoint` (≈25) stays. The task keeps geometry with the renderer until R5, and the plan's replacement (the Surface mapper) does not exist yet. The selection layer's private `findTextNode` is a near-twin, but reusing it would put a dependency on a method V4 is rewriting. (2) `jsonValuesEqual` stays in `awarenessSelection.ts` because `events/onInput.ts` and `session/editing/text.ts` use it for mark sets. Moving it would touch I2's files. (3) The plan's −330 for T6 exceeds L59's own ≈271. Even the full L59 (≈271) would leave T6 about 60 short of −330, and the part of the rest that is not geometry is already gone. **Census**: mechanism counts unchanged (setTimeout 37, rAF 8, `await tick()` 20, `flushMirror(` 15, `stopCapturing()` 1, `instanceof PreventionError` 1, `ignoreNextSelectionChange` 15, MutationObserver 4, `flushSync(` 0); type-body 932 → 932; vendor Δ +27 (unchanged). **Wire**: unchanged. It is still `selections[viewKey] = serialize(value) + t`, with `t` a client-local monotonic counter, and view keys are still `view-<n>`. Only the ownership of writes changed. A view whose teardown never runs at all (the object is leaked, never destroyed) keeps its entry until the awareness timeout or provider teardown, because nobody else sweeps it (R1). **Tests re-pinned because L59 is deleted**: in `presence.test.ts`, "sibling publish sweeps dead entries" is now two rows, own teardown without selection teardown (F-T10) and no sweep (R1); "clearAwarenessSelection remount" is now "clearing an absent key does not broadcast" and "teardown broadcasts once". In `elegance-awareness.test.ts`, "a swept map rebroadcasts" is now "a view's teardown rebroadcasts once" (the helper republishes through `publishPresence`). The comment in `lifecycle-dom.test.tsx` is updated. `docs/crdt-v14-selection.md` has a rewritten "Presence contract" section (it still described the pre-V2 legacy fields). **Deviations**: (1) the module keeps its path `collaboration/awarenessSelection.ts` rather than `sync/presence.ts`, for merge hygiene as at T2–T4; (2) the key lives on `Edytor` (the view), not on `EdytorSelection`, so the view's own teardown can clear it without the selection layer; (3) freshest-per-client still competes only among valid text entries, so a sibling view's newer block-set or atom entry does not hide an older caret. This is unchanged from V2's reading (c) and should be revisited when R5 draws block sets. |

The seeded tree equals the projection. Split, merge, move (to a new parent and back to the root), delete (with and without children), format, type and data, a nested batch, remote text and block inserts: after each, the tree equals a from-scratch build, the patch names exactly the affected cells (the moved cell is never among them, only the two parent lists), and every other cell keeps its object identity. One keystroke in 1,000 blocks patches one cell; 500 deletes in one transaction give one patch (F-O5, cell half). The K7 corpus: two replicas, both delivery orders, merged and single updates, undo/redo, nested batches, three client-id assignments, ranks and nonces from a seeded stream so a run is reproducible; after every commit the patched tree must equal a from-scratch build (3 seeds by default, `R1_CELLS_SEEDS=a-b` widens it: 1–1500 green). Segments: keyed by the preceding atom id or `start`; an edit inside a segment, an atom inserted before it, and an atom removed elsewhere rename no other segment. Render deltas merge equal marks by value (key order and `undefined` values ignored) and skip empty runs. Placeholder: one empty text and no live composition in it. **Owner** (`src/lib/surface/cells.ts`): `createCells(source, onPatch)` seeds from `project()` once and then applies each `onChange` report: removed subtrees dropped except children a changed parent still lists; added subtrees rebuilt with all their descendants; `order`, `meta` and `content` patched. A cell is a frozen value, and a patch replaces only the cells it names. `partsOf(runs)` (memoized per runs array), `renderDeltas`, `segmentDeltas(cell, segment, transform)` (a kind's `transformText` is fed declared values `{text: {stringContent, value}, block: {id, type, data}, content}`, not wrappers; F-P19's `transformText` half), `placeholderOf(cell, composing)`. Nothing renders from cells yet. **K7 defect found and fixed** (`crdt/text/runs.ts`, the D9 report). A block that was visible, moved into an added subtree and changed in the same commit, was reported only inside that subtree (the D9 contract: "an added subtree carries its descendants"). Its published baseline kept the old payload, so a later change back to the old type or text compared equal to the stale baseline and was not reported at all (the report came out empty). Before any fix, 115 of 300 seeds failed. Rebuilding an added subtree's descendants in the cells (they now do) brought that to 8 of 300; those 8 were this index defect. After the index fix, seeds 1–1500 are green. Today's mirror hides the defect because every op site runs a full mid-transaction `flushMirror`. Row "a block moved into an added subtree" (red without the fix: `expected [] to deeply equal [[['x'], ['x']]]`). F-O7's corpus never produced the sequence. **Shadow (rule 3)**. `compareView` (`src/tests/oracles/cells-render-model.ts`) reads the render model two ways. The mirror side is `Block.children`/`content`, `Text.renderChildren`, `isEmpty`, `endsWithNewline`, and `Text.svelte`'s placeholder condition without its DOM-read `hasDomText` half (L34, R5). The cell side is child ids, segment shape and atoms, each segment's render deltas (transform applied), filler and trailing newline, and the placeholder attribute. A second side compares the patched cells with a from-scratch build; any difference there is a cells bug and is never classed. Steps: the end of every synchronous turn in which a report was applied (one microtask compare), every `flushDomUpdates()`, and every test end. Views are discovered test-side: the lane's `beforeEach` wraps `Edytor.prototype.facade`, and a view's first read queues its registration for the end of the turn. Importing the runtime from the setup file instead changed module load order and broke the determinism probes' virtual clock. The same comparison runs in chromium on `/test/dom?cells=shadow` (`window.__EDYTOR_CELLS__`, `tests/editor-dom/cells-shadow.spec.ts`: typing, Enter at the end and in the middle, Backspace merge, Tab/Shift+Tab, bold, undo/redo, select-all delete, marks, atoms, lists, code lines with `transformText`). **Shadow difference census** (full dom lane, every DOM fixture and command program): 36 files, **5,912 steps, 21 differing, 0 unexplained**, 0 cells-vs-document differences. By class: (1) `composition-pin`, 19 steps in 3 files (`dom.test.ts` 13, `arch-v2-s3-marks` 4, `arch-v2-v2-select` 2) → **F-I10, F-I11 (I4)**. The live composition host renders the mirror's render pin, which lags or leads the committed runs the cell holds. Predicate: the differing part is the text currently serving its pin (`_pinnedDeltas && isComposing && compositionText === text`, `Text.renderChildren`'s own condition). Cells get the pin at I4 (`surface/pin`). (2) `placeholder-outside-composition-host`, 2 steps in 1 file (`adversarial-wave3`: a command inserts an empty block between `compositionstart` and commit) → **F-I15 / §2.4 placeholder attribute (R5)**. `Text.svelte` hides every placeholder while any composition is live; the attribute is withheld only in the composing cell. Predicate: a placeholder the mirror hides, in a block other than the composition host, while a composition is live. Chromium: 0 differences in every step. Mutation check: dropping the content patches makes both lanes fail at once (dom `command-programs` 87 of 122 steps unexplained; chromium at the first typed step). **Census** (mechanisms): unchanged: setTimeout 37, rAF 8, `await tick()` 20, `flushMirror(` 15, `stopCapturing()` 1, `instanceof PreventionError` 1, `ignoreNextSelectionChange` 15, MutationObserver 4, `flushSync(` 0. Type-body 932 → 965 (+33, the cell/segment/delta types). Vendor Δ +27 (unchanged). R1 deletes nothing (plan). **Removed at R4** (temporary, test-side): the oracle module, `cellsShadow.ts` and its setup/flush wiring, the route's `cells` parameter and `cells-shadow.spec.ts`. **Deviations**: (1) The comparison is between render models (what the mirror hands the components vs what cells give), not two rendered DOM trees. The live DOM also carries browser input the observer has not adopted, which is R6/R7's compare pass (revision 3), not R1's. The test route runs the same model comparison in chromium. (2) A production change outside `surface/`: the K7 fix in the D9 index (+5), because cells patched only from reports cannot be correct without it. (3) The corpus row and the K7 row were added in the implementation commit, after the corpus found the defect; the rest of the rows landed in the tests-first commit. (4) Cells rebuild every descendant of an added subtree, including previously visible blocks that did not change (new identities, same values), because the report does not say which of them changed. (5) Cells are plain frozen values with an `onPatch` callback, not `$state`. R2 makes them reactive when components read them. **Contract questions**: (a) Placeholder scope during a composition: today every empty block hides its placeholder while any composition is live; §2.4 hides it only in the composing cell. The census reads §2.4 as the contract (F-I15). Confirm, or make the attribute read "no live composition anywhere". (b) The D9 contract that an added subtree carries previously visible descendants without naming them again (as moved, retyped or edited) makes every consumer rebuild the whole subtree. Should the report instead name such ids in `moved`/`meta`/`content` (a cheaper, exact patch for R2's keyed components), or keep the contract and let R2 compare values to keep identities? |

I1a turns F-I1 and F-I5 green; I1b the rest. **Owner**: `session/attempt.ts` (204 xloc; §4.3 budget 510) — one inputType → intent table (`INTENTS`: kind, the key a binding sees, a delete's direction) with the misreport overrides (`intentOf`: `\n`/`\r` text insertions are line breaks; a line break reported for a key whose keydown announced another intent is that intent — Android Backspace as `insertParagraph`); the attempt value (`attemptOf`: intent, reported type, data/payload, declared range, cancelable, the keydown flag, and the **anchored target = the selection value**; the facts a command reads are projected from the target when it runs — `reproject` for the keydown attempt); the per-view queue `Attempts` (`admit`: a model-owned attempt supersedes every live one, a browser-owned one the browser-owned ones; `drift(attempt, mode, ms)`: the model owns the DOM drift around it — `refresh` / `restore` (caret) / `discard` (structure too) — until its deadline; `inputOf(type)`: the newest attempt whose expectation an `input` satisfies — a browser-owned one by compatible type, a model-owned one while it expects drift; `observed`: defer or discard observer records; `caret`: the command's caret for drift repair; `adopted(host)`: a browser-owned attempt's phase becomes `applied` when its host's change is adopted, by `input` or by the observer; `hold()`: a model write owns the DOM until the end of its task). Phases `open → applied | failed | closed`; `last` for diagnostics. `events/onBeforeInput`: admission → `occur(occurrence)` (declared range, attempt, ignore rules, owner rule `browserExpectation`, `perform` = one user command: bindings once per occurrence, then the drift expectation, the extension hook, the command, `failed` on a throw); `runOccurrence` for occurrences with no `beforeinput` (paste, drop, a native line break); `admitKeyAttempt` + the missing-`beforeinput` deadline (the key attempt re-selects its anchored target, is confirmed — closed — by a `beforeinput` of the same occurrence, and a target that no longer resolves is a named no-op, C10); the Android no-op-Backspace deadline asks "is the attempt still `open`", the caret still the anchored target and no native change pending, then deletes one grapheme before the anchored caret. `events/onInput`: `inputOf` attribution → `repairDrift` (model-owned) or adoption of the browser-owned attempt's expected change; when a model-owned attempt's drift is live on the same host and the DOM differs from the expected text, the expected text is adopted and the host re-rendered (F-I19). **Deleted**: L29 — `beforeInputSnapshot.ts` (the 25-field numeric snapshot; its facts are the target's projection), `beforeInputRepairTarget.ts`, the DOM-selection round trip for declared ranges (the declared range is resolved into the model selection directly; input/advanced-delete/drop/format specs green on three engines); `session/attempt.ts` +204. **Why 61 % short**: (1) S4 already booked ≈118 of L28 (the hotkey bridge's synthetic `KeyboardEvent` and 4 of 7 fabrication sites; S4 landed 118 over its own step); (2) the 11 `setSuppressedInputRepairSelectionTarget` sites became one-line `edytor.attempts.caret(…)` calls, not zero — the drift-repair caret is the command's result caret, which V3 (command result selections, parallel) makes a returned value; deleting them here would restructure selection writes; (3) the drift handling itself stays — the observer's defer/discard branch with its 16 ms retry, `onInput`'s repair (tick, the 20 ms wait for the discard pass, the 30 ms re-set) — each pinned by `mobile-beforeinput` specs; occurrences with no browser event do not reach it (no API change). (2) Offering non-cancelable line breaks to the key's bindings is accepted (F-I5); a binding that claims leaves the browser's drift to the observer, as a claimed cancelable key does. (3) A veto (`prevent()`) marks the attempt `failed`; the phases stay `open | applied | failed | closed` (L6), no `refused`. Unit: `src/tests/diffText.test.ts` (6: prefix/suffix, doubled letters, repeats, surrogates). The tests-first commit had a helper that counted the renderer's empty text anchors (the two F-I20 rows were red for the wrong reason); b43bd8d fixed it before the implementation and the rows were re-verified red on the reference. **Design** (O59, §4.4): `adopt(edytor, text, dom, domCaret)` in `events/domTextMutationObserver.ts` is the only adopter. It finds the open browser-owned attempt expecting the host (`Attempts.on(host)`, replaces `adopted`), `select()`s its anchored target (history's `before`), computes the preference from the live target (a collapsed backward delete ends at the target's caret; anything else starts there), diffs, selects the change position when no attempt's target is on the text, runs one user command `dispatcher.run(kind, …)` — `text.insertText({value, start, end})` (a replacement or insertion: hooks, `marksForInsertion`, pending marks) or `block.deleteContentAtRange` (a deletion) — sets the attempt's phase `applied`, or `failed` when a hook vetoed or replaced the change (then the text re-renders from the model), keeps a uniformly marked deletion's marks pending, and puts the caret where the browser put it, else at the change's end. The flush adopts claimed hosts **first**, before the structural repair (a browser-owned edit that removed a mark element is adopted, then re-rendered — as the old input-first ordering did); inside a model-owned attempt's drift deadline a claimed host is adopted with its expected text (F-I19) and the rest deferred; the selection the repair restores is re-captured after adoptions placed the caret. `utils/diffText.ts`: `diffText(before, after, caret)` → `{at, remove, insert}` — common prefix/suffix; inside a repeat the change slides to end at the caret (never splitting a surrogate pair); with no preference, after the longest common prefix. `onInput`: drift repair (model-owned) stays; otherwise the `input` flushes the queue (`observer.flushNow()`, which now takes pending records) and closes its attempt; an `input` no attempt expected (no `beforeinput` came) claims the text under the DOM caret as a browser-owned attempt of its own — the old second adopter's role, now an occurrence. `Expect.change` keeps `host` and `after` only. The Android no-op deadline asks "did this attempt's adoption apply (or refuse) a deletion" (`phase` `applied`/`failed`) instead of "is it still open": an `input` that closed the attempt without a change no longer loses the keystroke. **Deleted**: L31 — `reconcileBrowserOwnedInputTarget`, `reconcileFocusedDomText`, `reconcileDomText`/`reconcileTextValue`, `applyDomTextDiff` (raw `insertAt`/`deleteAt`), `insertNativeMentionTrigger` (core knowledge of `@`), `notifyBrowserOwnedTextInsertions` (manual `onAfterOperation`), `getCaretOffsetAfterTextDiff`, `planDomTextDiff`, the dead diff branch of the input-only range insertion, `isComposingInputEvent`, `getNativeLineBreakInsertionIndex`; L32 — `calculateSimilarity`, `advancedDiffText`, `myersDiff*`, `buildPath`, `simpleDiff`, `simpleArrayDiff`, `ultraFastDiffText` (replaced by the 25-xloc preferred-position diff); also the observer's copy of the DOM-text normalizer (`getNormalizedTextNodeContent` = `getNormalizedDomText`, reader F4). **Census** (vendor excluded): `await tick()` 20 → 18; setTimeout 36, rAF 8, `flushMirror(` 15, `stopCapturing()` 1, `instanceof PreventionError` 1, `ignoreNextSelectionChange` writes 15, MutationObserver 4, `flushSync(` 0 — unchanged; type-body 940 → 934; vendor Δ +27. **API**: `Edytor.observer` (new: the DOM observer; `flushNow()` returns its flush); `Attempts.on(host)` replaces `Attempts.adopted(host)`; `Expect.change` loses `before`/`caret`; `onInput.ts` no longer exports `reconcileTextValue`, `reconcileDomText`, `reconcileFocusedDomText`, `isLiveText`. Behavior: an adopted change is visible to `onBeforeOperation` (and vetoable) and reported once by `onAfterOperation` under `insertText` / `deleteContentAtRange`; an unattributed native deletion now cuts the undo capture like any deletion (policy table), where it used to coalesce. **Design iterations**: one — the first cut adopted claimed hosts inside the per-text loop, after `restoreRemovedManagedNodes`; `input-fallback` "preserves marks when reconciling a DOM text insertion" (an `input`-only engine that rewrote the whole text element) adopted the restored mark span too (`Hello!Hello`) and a replacement lost its caret to the pre-adoption anchor; claimed hosts now adopt first and the restored selection is re-captured. **Contract questions**: (1) an `input` with no attempt is admitted as a browser-owned attempt at the DOM caret's text (R8 "one occurrence, one attempt"): acceptable, or should a missing-`beforeinput` input stay unattributed and rely on the location default (text in a content is adopted) once R7's compare pass lands? (2) adoption shows hooks `insertText` with `start/end` for a replacement and `deleteContentAtRange` for a deletion — the same operations typed input uses; a hook that vetoes browser-made text gets its text re-rendered (reverted) — confirm this is the intended veto semantics for browser-owned input. (3) `beforeinput-fallback.spec.ts:422` (wrapper replacement with no input, caret 0 instead of 9) stays known-red: the caret, not the adoption, is wrong; left for R7's wrapper-replacement adoption. |

Browser `tests/editor-dom/arch-v2-v5-classifier.spec.ts` (6 × 3 engines): (a) mark toggle over `ea`, (b) a peer's format re-splits `lead` under the caret at 3 (then `X` → `leaXd`), (c) a gesture-less move right after typing (**red on all three**), a foreign write after typing adopted (C4), the IME post-commit jump reverted / adopted after the window — each asserting DOM = model now and 150 ms later. Mobile `mobile-arch-v2-v5-snapback.spec.ts`: after a real Backspace merge (`ab` + `tail`), a page-script caret at merge + 1 injected 60 ms later (past the merge's own reconciliation, inside the window) is snapped back on mobile-chromium and adopted on mobile-webkit (pin). cdp `arch-v2-v5-post-commit.cdp.spec.ts` (3, Chromium's real IME): after `noかte` commits the DOM caret is the model caret and stays; a `compositionend`-queued page-script jump to 0 is reverted; after the window a move is adopted (pins: the reference held them with timers). **Owner**: `surface/projector.svelte.ts` `classify(dom)` → `echo | drift | composition | foreign | intent`, over the last display (`#displayed` points + selection epoch), the render epoch (`#flushes` vs `#seen`: a flush after which the DOM selection was not observed, or DOM records the observer has not reconciled) and the gesture serial (`intentSerial` vs `#serial`, the serial at the last observation: a display, a clear, or an adopted/composition observation). Order: outside the editor or in a nested editable island → foreign (the adopter ignores it); our last display unchanged, or a text-bound range that already shows the value (replaces `nativeSelectionMatchesCurrentBlockSeam`) → echo; composing → composition (adopted as before — I3's); a requested display still to land with no gesture since the request → echo; no gesture and a named signature → drift; a gesture or a drag → intent; no gesture and a render → drift; else foreign. `onSelectionChange` = classify; echo returns, drift calls `display()` (the projector writes the current value after the flush), the rest go to `applySelectionSnapshot`. **Named, counted rules** (plan §9.1 rule 5; timestamps compared at use, no timer): `#snapBack` — Android Chrome, within 250 ms of a model-owned delete whose attempt was a collapsed caret at a text start (evidence `projector.deleted(attempt)` from `runBeforeInputDeleteCommand`, replacing `lastDeleteCommandAt` and the arming at six write sites), no gesture since, the value a collapsed caret in another text, the DOM caret one right of it in that text; `#jump` — within 100 ms of a composition commit (`projector.committed()` from `stabilizeCompositionSelection`), no gesture since. **Echo-after-clear derived away**: with the display's serial recorded at the clear and the pointer presses cancelled, no lane needed it (chromium, firefox, webkit, both mobile projects over inline-atomic, selection, hotkeys, navigation-selection-sync, beforeinput-fallback, structural-keys, plugins, mobile-beforeinput, mobile-composition: 278 + 221 passed with it removed, then every full lane) — a clear now just records the observation. **Pointer-originated ignores → the pointer cancels the native default**: block chrome (`handleNonNativeEditableBlockChromePointerDown`) `preventDefault()`s its pointerdown (a canceled pointerdown moves no selection on the three desktop engines — probed) and focuses the host itself; a native control inside the chrome (a void block's `<input>`) keeps its press; atoms already cancelled theirs (their two setters simply go); a triple click cancels the native multi-click at `mousedown` (`preventNativeTripleClick`, `detail ≥ 3`) and `click` selects the block content immediately (its rAF + `setTimeout` deferrals go); `onKeyDown`'s refresh no longer reads the flag. **Deleted** (L23): `ignoreNextSelectionChange` (7 writes + resets + the `onKeyDown` read), `ignoreNextSelectedBlockSelectionChange` (never set true), `nativeSelectionMatchesCurrentBlockSeam`, `restoreDriftedEchoCaret` + `lastEchoGestureSerial`, `restorePostDeleteShiftedCaret` + `recordPostDeleteCaretTarget` + `postDeleteCaretTarget` + `lastDeleteCommandAt` + `caretSignature`/`#caret` + `ANDROID_POST_DELETE_RESTORE_WINDOW_MS`, `domSelectionChurnSeq` + `markDomSelectionChurn` (6 call sites) + `churnBaselineAtGesture` (the render epoch is the projector's flush count), the projector's `awaited`/`isEcho`/`renderedSinceObservation` (folded into `classify`) and its `cleared` display record, `stabilizeCompositionSelection`'s restore closure with its rAF, 0 ms and 30 ms re-writes + `clearCompositionSelectionRestore` + the two fields (the function keeps its name and signature: release the pin, guard foreign/island focus, arm `#jump`, select the caret). **Census** (ref → V5): `setTimeout(` 28 → 24 · rAF 8 → 5 · `await tick()` 12 · `flushMirror(` 15 · `stopCapturing()` 1 · `instanceof PreventionError` 1 · **`ignoreNextSelectionChange` writes 7 → 0** · MutationObserver 4 · `flushSync(` 0 · type-body 967 · vendor 7,152 (Δ +27, unchanged) · **named browser rules 5** (Android no-op-Backspace deadline and missing-`beforeinput` deadline in `events/`, the model-owned drift deadline in `session/attempt`, and the classifier's two signatures — no third). **Deviations**: (1) *Records-pending clause is drift, not ignore*: V4's `awaited` ignored a gesture-less move while the observer held records, which left DOM ≠ model forever when the records were our own render's (deferred by a model-owned attempt's deadline: the red row). It is now drift (displayed again); a host write inside that ≈16 ms reconciliation window is therefore reverted — the F-S10 foreign-write rows write after settle. (2) *IME window 100 ms*: the reference re-wrote at sync, rAF, 0 ms and 30 ms; the timestamp rule covers a jump up to 100 ms after the commit and adopts after it. (3) *`composition.spec.ts:565` stays red on purpose*: its "delayed jump" is placed with `setSelectionByTextIndex`, which marks a user gesture (the harness has done so since before G0), so V5 — like the reference's gesture-disarmed timers — adopts it; the gesture-less jump is the V5 browser and cdp rows (green). (4) *Mechanical test updates*: `selection-write-dedupe` (two tests set the retired flag: now "the next real selectionchange is adopted"), `android-caret-restore.test.tsx` deleted (it merged through `mergeBlockBackward` and stamped `lastDeleteCommandAt` by hand; its three cases are V5 rows driven through the real delete path), the DST settle probe stops reading the deleted `compositionSelectionRestoreFrame`, a `test.utils` comment. (5) *Composition*: a `selectionchange` while composing is classified `composition` and still adopted as on the reference. **For I3**: `stabilizeCompositionSelection` is now 20 lines (pin release, focus guards, `projector.committed()`, `setAtTextOffset`) — the session end should call `projector.committed()` where it commits and may fold the function into the session; whether a composition `selectionchange` is adopted or ignored ("composition noise") is the session's decision (`classify` returns `composition` first when `isComposing`); `driftAfterComposition`'s 50 ms attempt, the dangling-blur timer and `compositionEndedAt` are untouched; F-S12's restart half stays I3's. |
