| P2.8 fix: index invalidation | branch `arch-v2-fix-index` on 34270c0 (this commit) | 19,675 → 19,674 (**−1**) | `R1_CELLS_SEEDS=1-1500`: **1,516/1,516** (was 33 red) · `R1_CELLS_SEEDS=1-3000`: **3,016/3,016** (1 more red, seed 2999, before the second fix) · `p28-index-invalidation` 12/12 (red before: 12) · Sequential: `check` 0/0 · `lint` clean · `check:worker` OK · `vitest --run` 154 files (3 skipped), 2,806 passed, 3 expected-fail, 3 skipped, 11 todo · `test:crdt` 133 files (2 skipped), 2,680 passed, 3 expected-fail, 9 skipped (seed-37/59 json restored, no stray `seed-*.doc.json`; the d6 and p1-scale timing rows passed in the full run) · `test:dom` 51 files (1 skipped), 785 passed, 1 expected fail, 33 skipped · `test:do` 23/23 · Playwright/DST: not run (orchestrator) | **Root cause (not a placement write)**: a diagnostic compared every live block's cached `runs(b)` with a fresh display after every commit, over seeds 1–3,000; the first stale block of each of the 33 red seeds (plus seed 1155, stale on a block not visible at the time) fell in two groups, both *a claim the display walk skipped, then became effective*. `display(x)` follows a claim `{m}` on `x`'s list only when `m` is live and `x` is `m`'s top claimer; the walk tracked only the blocks it followed, so `x` was not a list consumer of a skipped `m`. **(A) revival**, 19 seeds (73, 132, 176, 193, 194, 330, 380, 406, 493, 508, 610, 685, 749, 754, 970, 1114, 1320, 1374, 1445): `x` claims a deleted `m` (a concurrent delete and merge); undo removes `m`'s delete mark, so `x` now shows `m`. The fold invalidated `m` and its consumers, not `x`. **(B) claimer hand-over**, 14 seeds (97, 253, 423, 432, 528, 628, 647, 808, 864, 865, 871, 1093, 1234, 1365; + 1155): `m`'s winning claim is held by `z`; `z`'s claim goes away (undo of its merge: 8; a remote claims change: 1, seed 423; a local split moving the claim to the new block: 1, seed 647) or `z` is deleted by a remote delivery (4: 253, 864, 1093, 1365), so `x` becomes `m`'s top claimer. The fold invalidated `effects(z)` = `m` and `m`'s consumers, not `x`. The P2.8 row's reading (seed 73 = `moveBlock`) was the commit where the stale cell became visible; the stale runs dated from an earlier undo of `n73-78`'s delete. Placement is not an input of `display` and is still not invalidated. **Fix (one rule, `text/runs.ts` `computeFresh`)**: a cached display depends on every claim on every list it walked, followed or skipped: the dependency capture adds each claim target to `deps.lists`. Any structural fold of `m` (delete mark, entry, claims) and any claim change of another holder of `m` (`effects(z)` ∋ `m` → `listConsumers(m)`) now invalidates `x` and names it in the report. The extra entries are the claims `displayOf` already iterates, so the fold stays linear per transaction. **Second cause (found by 1–3000, seed 2999; pre-existing, same owner)**: the report's `removed` named only the ids whose before-ancestors all stayed visible. A block that leaves while its before-parent survives (commit 52: `a` removed, its child `a1` re-parented to the root, `a1`'s child `n2999-0` deleted) was covered by `a`'s removal, but the cells' drop stops at the surviving `a1`, so `n2999-0`'s subtree kept orphan cells. When `box` (in that subtree) became visible again with remote edits made while hidden, K7 kept the stale orphan cell. Fix: a removed id is a root when its before-parent stays visible (or is the root), which is O(1) per id instead of a walk up. The test oracle `diffSnaps` (`src/tests/oracles/doc-change.ts`) had the same rule and got the same change (contract reading: `removed` = the roots of the removed forest in the before tree; consumers drop a root's subtree through its old child lists, stopping at children still listed). **Rows** (`src/tests/crdt/arch-v2/p28-index-invalidation.test.ts`, three client assignments each, facade + index + report, expected values from `project()`): (A) an undone delete of a claimed block shows in its claimer; (B) undoing the winning merge hands the claimed block to the other claimer, locally and on the peer; (B) a remote delete of the winning claimer hands it over; (C) a block removed under a surviving moved parent is a removed root, and after a hidden remote edit and an undo its cell shows the edit. All 12 red before (A/B at the cached-runs assertion; C at `removed`, and at the cell text with that assertion dropped). **Bench** (`bench:crdt` p50 before → after, load average ≈ 7–8; v13 lanes, untouched, moved +5–20 %): keystroke facade 100/1k/5k 0.083/0.547/2.45 → 0.091/0.527/2.52 ms (1k write 0.067 → 0.062, commit 0.037 → 0.036, runs 0.020 → 0.020, 37 B); claims-128 0.139 → 0.106 ms; ops delete/paste-text/paste-subtree/format 0.539/0.558/1.47/0.592 → 0.540/0.527/1.41/0.530 ms; split/merge 0.302/0.108 → 0.306/0.119 ms; remote burst apply 0.0058 → 0.0064 ms; history fresh/fragmented 0.60/0.56 → 0.49/0.57 ms. Noise; no lane worse beyond it. |
| P5 Durable Object room (`edytor/cloudflare`) | a975d85 (P5.2 protocol), 8364c5b (P5.1 export), 8852924 (P5.4 rows), d069e3d (packed smoke), docs + this ledger commit | 19,413 → 19,979 (**+566**: `src/lib/cloudflare` 458, `crdt/providers` +108; plan ≈ +305) | Sequential on d069e3d (the docs commit changes no code): `check` 0/0 · `lint` clean · `check:worker` OK for both entries (crdt 863.8 KiB, cloudflare 880.6 KiB unminified) · `vitest --run` 151 files (3 skipped), 2,784 passed, 3 expected-fail, 3 skipped, 11 todo · `test:crdt` 130 files (2 skipped), 2,772 passed, 3 expected-fail, 9 skipped (seed-37/59 json restored) · `test:dom` 47 files (1 skipped), 721 passed, 33 skipped · `test:typecheck` 0/0 · `test:dom:typecheck` 0/0 · `test:do` 23/23 (3 consecutive runs) · `test:do:typecheck` 0 · `test:hosted` chromium 9/9, firefox 8/8 (+1 skipped: the cross-engine row runs from chromium), webkit 8/8 (+1 skipped) · `tests/packed-consumer/run.sh` all pass · arch chromium `collaboration` specs 26/26 (provider change). DST not run. | **Export.** `DocumentRoom` (Durable Object; the proof coordinator's logic moved in: generation admission, schema refusal, append-only chunked SQLite rows in one `transactionSync`, restore under `blockConcurrencyWhile`, instance-free presence with per-socket attachments, `noTimers` on every entry point) + `routeDocumentSocket(request, namespace, documentId, authorize)` + `requestedReplica`. **Identity.** Authorize before the upgrade (403/426), forward only `X-Edytor-User/Replica/Access`; the room binds `{user, replica, readOnly}` in the attachment, registers client ids to users (`replicas` table) and refuses 1008 an update with new structs under another user's id (or an unregistered id with history); a replica owned by another user is refused 403 at the dial; presence only for the socket's replica; read-only sockets get `permission-denied` per write, are never sent a Step1, and stay open. **Ack.** `messageSaved` (4) = room state vector after every sync message, sent after the write; provider `saved`/`unsaved`/`'saved'` from per-update state vectors (no timer). **Catch-up.** `messageChunk` (5) start/part/end above `EDYTOR_MAX_FRAME_BYTES` (32 MiB); the provider reassembles per socket and reads only complete frames. **Compaction** `mergeUpdates` of the stored records after `EDYTOR_COMPACT_AFTER` (500) update records, and over RPC. tests/do moved to the export (`tests/do/room.ts` deleted); rows added: authorize/403/426/header stripping/401, forged client id (1008 + 403), reloaded replica replays offline edits (other user refused), presence binding, read-only, saved signal with eviction restore, ack = stored state vector, chunk codec, chunked catch-up (raw + shipped) and chunked live broadcast, auto-compaction. tests/hosted (+3 per engine): Node socket forging a page's client id, saved/unsaved in a page through offline edits + eviction, chunked live update and late-joiner catch-up measured on page frames (frame limit 16 KiB). Packed Worker smoke imports `edytor/cloudflare`. **Deviations**: (1) the client-id rule is per USER, not per socket: "only the socket's replica" would refuse a reloaded page's offline edits (a new `doc.clientID` delivering the old one's structs from IndexedDB) and cross-tab forwarding — both hosted rows; the socket's `replica` is still registered to its user at the dial so nobody else can take it. (2) `replica` is optional in `authorize`'s answer: without it the socket's first presence entry binds it (the shipped provider announces `doc.clientID` in its hello; the test route cannot pass `?replica=`). (3) Clients do not chunk their own frames (client → room stays capped at 32 MiB by the platform). (4) Edits outside the stated scope: `eslint.config.js` + `scripts/check-worker-bundle.mjs` (the room joins the Worker-safe set; only it may import `cloudflare:workers`), `src/cloudflare-workers.d.ts` (type-check-only shim outside `src/lib`), `crdt/providers/room.ts` (message types + chunk codec, shared by room and provider). (5) +566 xloc vs ≈ +305 planned: identity/ownership (~60) and the knobs were not in the estimate. |

### Phase 2 release notes (collected)

- P5: `edytor/cloudflare` (new export): `DocumentRoom` Durable Object + `routeDocumentSocket` + `requestedReplica`; deploy per README "Server coordinator" (binding `new_sqlite_classes: ["DocumentRoom"]`, route, `authorize → {userId, replica?, readOnly?}`). Replaces the hand-written coordinator.
- P5: the room refuses (1008) an update writing new structs under a client id another user owns; clients should send `replica=<doc.clientID>` in `params`.
- P5: `WebsocketProvider` gains `saved`, `unsaved` and a `'saved'` event (room acknowledgements, `messageSaved` = 4) and reassembles chunked frames (`messageChunk` = 5); `edytor/crdt/edytor` exports `messageSaved`, `messageChunk`, `MAX_FRAME_BYTES`, `chunkFrame`, `createChunkReader`. Older clients report the new types through `'message-error'`; small documents sync unchanged.
